# OCR: Nemesis/Оригинальная игра, Nemesis/Комнаты, Nemesis/Тайлы

Для русских правил и некоторых карточек существуют более новые версии и ручные расшифровки: см. [README](README.md).

Сгенерировано `tools/ocr_texts.py` (RapidOCR). Порядок текста — по карточкам сетки, если лист разбивается на ячейки. Черновое машинное распознавание без проверки: текст может быть пропущен, искажён или перепутан между карточками; значки/иконки и кириллица часто распознаются неверно. Обязательно сверяйте важные правила с исходным изображением. Тексты карт, каких видит игрок в движке, — в `NEMESIS_CARD_TEXTS.md` и `NEMESIS_DLC_REFERENCE.md`; здесь — печатные оригиналы.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/072AE61487FC63A7F45B385031B1650696F3A7DF_Room_EvacA.png`

EVACUATION SECTION A
If any Escape Pod in Section A
is unlocked:
Make a Noise Roll. If no sappears
in the Room, enter an unlocked Pod
in Section A.
AWAKEN REALMS

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/0A66AECCE98E6500B6168C2C798414A1D1C994C7_Room_Airlock.png`

AIRLOCK CONTROL
Choose any 1 Room with a Technical
Corridors Entrance, a , and without
Destroyed Doors in nearby Corridors.
Close all nearby Doors to this Room, and
place the Airlock Procedure token in it.
Characters in there are unpassed.
REALMS AWAKEN

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/0FEB8CEFA2239D9D73FCDBC69F414CD1A53286BE_Room_Engine.png`

ENGINE ROOM
Secretly look at the top Engine token
for each Engine Room.
You don't need to tell the truth about
what you saw. The top Engine token
indicates the current state of the Engine.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/2CB3F7DD635308E7DDAF95C61995E108BCE03FA4_Room_Storage.png`

STORAGE
Draw 1 Item of each type: ,", and
Keep 1 and discard the others.
Then, make a Noise roll.
11
AWAKEN REALMS

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/30595B12DAE902F753CE273C3B478BF4B8E8C835_Room_Surgery.png`

SURGERY
Discard all A from your deck and hand.
Then, remove all Contamination cards
from your discard (without Scanning
them). If you have a Larva, remove it.
Lose 1 and Pass.
+++

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/3B7BD65338A86F5D176D5F47223C6D0753856CDD_Room_CommandCenter.png`

COMMAND CENTER
Choose any 1 Room and Close and/or
Open any Doors in Corridors connected
to this Room.
H
REALMS AWAKEN

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/3FCF1ECB07F01992F4D05805138F485219CB0381_Room_Contaminated.png`

CONTAMINATED ROOM
Passive: Gain a Contamination if you
use a "Search" Action in this Room,
or otherwise gain an Item here.
X
m

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/47F53F920D0F759B9E1F234AF6A8C3C734D4FB17_Room_Nest.png`

NEST
Passive: The first player to Explore this
Room gains 1 Knowledge.
Take or destroy 1 Egg from the Laboratory
Board, then make a Noise roll.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/506E6F3523CAAB8A0F4260DC4EC2519C3FECA59A_Room_HatchControl.png`

HATCH CONTROL SYSTEM
Flip 1 Escape Pod token to its
Locked or Unlocked side.
+
1 LOCKED
REALMS AWAKEN

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/565ED130923FF1DFE1DD81E1A148F38FC43A4622_Room_Containment.png`

CONTAINMENT CONTROL
Place an Adult in any 1 Room
with a Technical Corridor Entrance
and without a .
Place a in the Containtment
ControlRoomafterwards.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/63375AFF44CF4BE7E0F99930C6E55F8321633548_Room_Server.png`

SERVER ROOM
Use the Room Action of any 1 other
Explored Room with a
(even with a ).
Reveal a new Computer card.
If it's Lockdown, resolve it in this Room.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/6991889A743A908E03116AF47126009FE10346EC_Room_Comms.png`

COmmS rOOM
Send a Signal.
AWAKEN REALMS

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/7419CD85EC4DB14A7361CD083BDAEFB8AEF417CD_Room_Decon.png`

DECON ROOM
Remove all Contamination cards from
your hand without Scanning them.
If you have a Larva, remove it.
+++

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/9389F47F80C00E93DD1C87826E825A86C6651354_Room_Generator.png`

GENERATOR
Start/stop the Autodestruct Procedure.
AutodestructProcedure:
TaketheAutodestruct tokenandplaceit 3rounds
forwardontheTimeTrack(thereshouldbe2slots
empty between the Time token and Autodestruct token).
Place itwith theYellowsideup.
REALMS AWAKEN

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/9D3B23639100358CDE8A7A996BB5443F3A38F1A3_Room_Cafeteria.png`

CAFETERIA
Restore 2 and/or remove 1
Contamination card from your
hand without Scanning it.
Passive: Draw 1 additional A
atthestartoftheround.
+++

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/A1DBFA36479E669560EED428921BE01088975DBE_Room_Archives.png`

ARCHIVES
If you have an inactive Knowledge token:
Gain 2 Knowledge and flip your
Knowledge token to the active side.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/AE77E219D71597B654F6E85322156D57FCEF120C_Room_Repository.png`

REPOSITORY
If the Support space on your board isn't marked:
Draw 3 Items from the Support Equipment deck.
You may keep 1 of them and place the rest at the
bottom of the deck. Then, place a Universal marker
on the Support space of your board.

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/B08D634539003F98DD8E31CF5896807A4038D47E_Room_Lab.png`

LABORATORY
Analyze a Heavy Object in the Room or
carried by YOUR Character. Gain 2 Knowledge.
If the Object type has not yet been Analyzed,
place it above any chosen Adaptation and
discard theAdaptation,otherwise
discard theHeavyObject.
+++

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/B395DEEB432938CBB7B6DA7858573835820CBC9C_Room_FireControl.png`

FIRE CONTROL SYSTEM
Choose 1 Room. Discard a
from that Room (if there is one).
Repel all from that Room.
REALMS AWAKEN

### `Nemesis/Комнаты/E144A15BA6365770A1DE9AC5C9FF7BE911CC32B9_Room_Armory.png`

ARMORY
Load up to 2 lll in a
Ranged weapon you carry.
m
